Adds the technical details needed to describe data exchanges between systems: exchange pattern, 
transport protocol, payload specifications, security requirements, and relationships to other 
transactions. This model is technology-agnostic — it can describe exchanges based on FHIR, 
HL7 v2, CDA, DICOM, X12, SOAP, or any other standard.">Transaction</a><a name="ru-Transaction"> </a></td><td style="vertical-align: top; text-align : var(--ig-left,left); background-color: white; border: 0px #F0F0F0 solid; padding:0px 4px 0px 4px" class="hierarchy"/><td style="vertical-align: top; text-align : var(--ig-left,left); background-color: white; border: 0px #F0F0F0 solid; padding:0px 4px 0px 4px" class="hierarchy"><span style="opacity: 0.5">0</span><span style="opacity: 0.5">..</span><span style="opacity: 0.5">*</span></td><td style="vertical-align: top; text-align : var(--ig-left,left); background-color: white; border: 0px #F0F0F0 solid; padding:0px 4px 0px 4px" class="hierarchy"><a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html">Interaction</a></td><td style="vertical-align: top; text-align : var(--ig-left,left); background-color: white; border: 0px #F0F0F0 solid; padding:0px 4px 0px 4px" class="hierarchy">Transaction<br/><br/>Элементы, определенные в предках:<a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="Unique identifier(s) for this interaction definition.">identifier</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="A short human-readable label for the interaction (e.g., 'Patient Demographics Query').">name</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="A display title for the interaction.">title</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="A human-readable description of what this interaction accomplishes.">description</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="The type of actors involved.">type</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="The publication status of this interaction definition.">status</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="The date this interaction definition was last changed.">date</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="Why this interaction is defined — the business or clinical need it addresses.">purpose</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="The actor that triggers the interaction (logical source).">initiator</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="The actor that receives and responds to the interaction (logical endpoint).">responder</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="Additional actors involved in the interaction beyond initiator and responder.">participant</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="A description of the event or condition that initiates this interaction.">trigger</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="Conditions that must be true before this interaction can occur.">preCondition</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="Conditions that are expected to be true after this interaction completes successfully.">postCondition</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="The outbound payload — what the initiator supplies.">request</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="The return payload — what the responder returns. Absent for fire-and-forget patterns.">response</a>, <a href="StructureDefinition-Interaction.html" title="The processing semantics of the interaction — what actors do during the exchange, beyond what is expressed in pre/postConditions and the payload structure.">semantics</a></td></tr>

               value="A system-to-system technical exchange contract, extending the Interaction model. 
Adds the technical details needed to describe data exchanges between systems: exchange pattern, 
transport protocol, payload specifications, security requirements, and relationships to other 
transactions. This model is technology-agnostic — it can describe exchanges based on FHIR, 
HL7 v2, CDA, DICOM, X12, SOAP, or any other standard."/>

                  value="May be used to represent additional information that is not part of the basic definition of the element and that modifies the understanding of the element in which it is contained and/or the understanding of the containing element's descendants. Applications processing a resource are required to check for modifier extensions.

Modifier extensions SHALL NOT change the meaning of any elements on Resource or DomainResource (including cannot change the meaning of modifierExtension itself)."/>

Though any implementer can define an extension, there is a set of requirements that SHALL be met as part of the definition of the extension. Applications processing a resource are required to check for modifier extensions.

Modifier extensions SHALL NOT change the meaning of any elements on Resource or DomainResource (including cannot change the meaning of modifierExtension itself)."/>

    <element id="Transaction">
      <path value="Transaction"/>
      <short value="Transaction"/>
      <definition
                  value="A system-to-system technical exchange contract, extending the Interaction model. 
Adds the technical details needed to describe data exchanges between systems: exchange pattern, 
transport protocol, payload specifications, security requirements, and relationships to other 
transactions. This model is technology-agnostic — it can describe exchanges based on FHIR, 
HL7 v2, CDA, DICOM, X12, SOAP, or any other standard."/>
